- **Never edited:** `evidence.mjs` (`codexHookVerdicts`, `oneLine`, `markdownCell`, `computeArtifactInputs`, `validateContainer` are imported), `artifact.mjs`, `probe-items.mjs`, `claude-baseline.mjs` (never imported).

**F6.4 `authorLoopStory` home and contract (replaces M6:688-692).**
- It lives in the new `scripts/clean-room/loop-script.mjs`. It imports `stripInstructionsBlock` from `./codex-hooks.mjs` and `locateFrontmatterFence` from `../../scaffold/core/.cleargate/scripts/assert_story_files.mjs` (import-safe: its `isMain` guard is at :401-402).
- Putting it in `probe-items.mjs` would need a `probe-items → codex-hooks → probe-items` import cycle, and it would pull the Codex harness into `claude-baseline.mjs`'s import graph.
- Body: `const s = stripInstructionsBlock(raw); const f = locateFrontmatterFence(s); if (!f) throw new Error('codex-loop: frontmatter not found'); return s.slice(0, f.end) + f.eol + f.eol + bodyMarkdown;`
- An absent `<instructions>` block throws stripInstructionsBlock's own message (`codex-hooks: anchor not found: <instructions> block`), never re-wrapped.
- `bodyMarkdown` is `fixtures/codex-loop/story-body.md`, which ends in `\n`.

**F6.5 Sessions, prompts and routes (replaces M6:715-727 and the S1–S5 bullets at M6:768-783).**
- Six sessions, each a new root thread through `runLoopTurn` with the AD-M6-9 posture, run in this order: S1, then `approveProbeStory` + commit, S2a, S2b, remove `src/cg-probe.ts` (recorded), kickoff (M6:772-776 unchanged, card text unchanged), S3, `touch .processed-<cardHash(card)>`, S4, S5.
- **Probe epic.** `cleargate new epic cleanroom-probe` → `EPIC-001_cleanroom_probe.md` (was `loop-probe`), then `authorProbeEpic`.
- **Marker set.** `CG-LOOP-ORCHESTRATOR`, `CG-CONTROL-EDIT`, `CG-CONTROL-INGEST`, `CG-CONTROL-SPAWN-DENY`, `CG-CONTROL-SPAWN-ALLOW`, `CHILD_TASK`, `ROLE=architect`, `ROLE=qa-red`, `ROLE=developer`, `ROLE=qa-verify`, `ROLE=devops`.
- **Route order.** Root/control markers, then `CHILD_TASK`, then the `ROLE=` markers.

| Session | Maps to `codexHookVerdicts` | Live prompt (one line) | Scripted steps (one tool action per step, then a final message) |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 edit deny (no `.active`, story unapproved) | `turns.C3`, `c3:{probeExists, warnLogLines}` | `CG-CONTROL-EDIT Use apply_patch (not a shell command) to create src/cg-probe.ts containing exactly "export const cgProbe = true;". Do nothing else.` | apply_patch Add `<roomReal>/src/cg-probe.ts` (`export const cgProbe = true;`) → `DONE` |
| S2a edit allow (story approved) | `turns.C4`, `c4:{probeExists}` | same prompt as S1 | same as S1 |
| S2b ingest + path probe | `turns.C2`, `c2:{gateCheckLine, epicRaw}` (session-start + post-tool-use) | `CG-CONTROL-INGEST Use apply_patch to replace "Hardcode it." with "Hardcode it for now." in .cleargate/delivery/pending-sync/EPIC-001_cleanroom_probe.md, then run the shell command "command -v cleargate" and report its output. Do nothing else.` | apply_patch Update `<roomReal>/.cleargate/delivery/pending-sync/EPIC-001_cleanroom_probe.md` (`- **Human Answer:** Hardcode it.` → `- **Human Answer:** Hardcode it for now.`) → exec `command -v cleargate` → `DONE` |
| S3 dispatch deny (a pending card, `.active` set) | `turns.C5`, `c5:{pendingCodexSentinels}` | `CG-CONTROL-SPAWN-DENY Call spawn_agent exactly once with agent_type "qa", task_name "cg_probe", fork_turns "none" and message "STORY=001-01 Reply with exactly OK.", then reply DONE. Use no other tool.` | spawn `{task_name:"cg_probe", agent_type:"qa", fork_turns:"none", message:CHILD_TASK}` → `DONE` (no `wait_agent` after a blocked spawn) |
| S4 dispatch allow + identity (card processed) | `turns.C6`, `c6:{tokenLedgerLogLines, pendingCodexSentinels, ledgerRowsBefore, ledgerRowsAfter, expectedAgentType:'qa', requests}` | `CG-CONTROL-SPAWN-ALLOW` + the S3 instruction, then `wait_agent until it answers, then reply DONE.` | spawn (same args) → wait_agent `{timeout_ms:120000}` → `DONE`; the probe child (`CHILD_TASK` route) answers `OK` |
| S5 loop | `codexLoopVerdicts` loop half | M6:780-781 verbatim | M6:721-727 with each spawn message `STORY=001-01 ROLE=<role> <one-line brief>` (the sentinel's work item is `STORY-001-01`); the record pipeline M6:729-731 verbatim, run with workdir = room root (no `--repo`) |

- **Every tool action in every scripted route is a code-mode `exec`**, exactly as M6:710-713 (X1, X4); spawns and waits stay top-level `collaboration` function calls. Item builders:
  - spawns use `spawnAgentItem` (exported, F6.3);
  - final messages use `assistantMessage`;
  - `exec` custom-tool items follow the same `{type:'custom_tool_call', id, call_id, name:'exec', input:<js>, status:'completed'}` shape as `applyPatchItem`.
- **Inputs to `codexHookVerdicts`.**
  - `registrations = readCodexRegistrations(room)`.
  - `trust`: as posture.
  - `c6.tokenLedgerLogLines`: only the lines appended to `.cleargate/hook-log/token-ledger.log` during the graded S4 attempt (a window, so a second attempt or the loop never adds a second identity line).
  - `c2.gateCheckLine = readLastGateCheckLine(room, /file=.*EPIC-001_cleanroom_probe\.md$/)` read after S2b; `c3.warnLogLines = readWarnLogLines(room)` read after S1.
  - `c6.requests` = the mock requests of S4 (scripted) or `[]` (live).
- **Attempts** (live only; scripted runs exactly 1 per session). A control session gets a second attempt, on a fresh thread with the preconditions restored, iff its first attempt produced zero `preToolUse` hook runs (the model made no gated tool call). The graded attempt is the last one run, and the evidence records the attempt count per session.

**F6.6 `codexLoopVerdicts` — reuse plus two overlays (extends M6:788-809).**
- **Raw input.** Top-level keys:
  - `provider`;
  - `trust: { posture, trusted, listing, configWrites, configBefore, configAfter }`;
  - `controls` (the exact `codexHookVerdicts` input of F6.5);
  - `attempts`;
  - `loop: { rootThreadId, notifications, identityLines, rollouts: {[agentId]: text}, commands: [{threadId, command, exitCode}], commandSource, gitWorktreeList, gitBranches, mergeCommit: {sha, parents}, storyHeadSha, lsTree, stateJson, eventsJsonl, serialLog, recordExitCode, importProbe, npmTestExit, served }`;
  - `pathIdentity: { hookSide, roleShell, expected }`;
  - `roomLedgers: {[rel]: text}`;
  - `metaRepoAppended: {[rel]: text}`;
  - `verifyInstallExit`, `doctorExit`.

  All raw strings, arrays or numbers. The only booleans are -10's filesystem facts (`probeExists`), per flashcard 183.
- **Control ids.** The seven `codex.live.*` ids come from `codexHookVerdicts(inputs.controls)`, unchanged. Two overlays apply on top:
  1. **Human posture.** `codex.live.discovery` := `humanTrustDiscovery(registrations, trust.listing)` (a new pure export). It PASSes iff every registration has at least one listing entry with the same eventName/matcher/command, and every such entry is `source:'project'`, `enabled:true`, `trustStatus:'trusted'`. FAIL details are `missing: <Event> <command>` / `untrusted: <Event> <command>`. Reason: the owner trusts the hooks before `run`, so no untrusted listing is observable (flashcard 107).
  2. **Negative controls.** `codex.live.edit-deny` := `INCONCLUSIVE` iff the graded S1 attempt has zero `preToolUse` runs and `c3.probeExists === false`; detail `no gated tool call in <n> attempt(s); src/cg-probe.ts absent`. `codex.live.dispatch-deny` := `INCONCLUSIVE` iff the graded S3 attempt has zero `preToolUse` runs and no `subAgentActivity` item; detail `no gated tool call in <n> attempt(s); no subagent started`. In every other case the reused verdict stands; for example, zero runs with the file present stays FAIL.
- **Join rules.**
  - **`codex.loop.hook-matrix`**: a hook run belongs to an item when `run.id` ends with `:<item.id>` (X5, and -10's `codexPostToolUseVerdict` rule) and it is in the same `threadId`.
    - A `fileChange` item with `status:'completed'` needs a completed `preToolUse` and a completed `postToolUse`.
    - A blocked item needs a `blocked` `preToolUse` and no `postToolUse`.
    - A spawn needs a dispatch-gate `preToolUse`.
    - A dispatch needs a completed `subagentStop`.
    - A root session needs `sessionStart` completed exactly once.
  - **Measure first, in pass B's first scripted run:** whether code-mode-nested `apply_patch` produces `fileChange` items whose `id` equals the hook `run.id` suffix. If it does not, the Developer stops with a spec-gap blockers report quoting the measured ids (Architect amends); no join is invented.
  - **`commands`**: taken from `item/completed` items of type `commandExecution`, keyed by `params.threadId` (`commandSource: 'items'`). If pass B's first scripted run shows that nested `exec_command` calls produce no `commandExecution` item, they are taken from the per-thread rollouts (`commandSource: 'rollouts'`). The evidence prints the source, and the dev report records the measured shape.
- **`codex.loop.dispatches`** joins each dispatch to exactly one identity line by `agent_id` = child `threadId`, and checks the line's `agent_type` against the rollout `session_meta.agent_role`. Work item is recorded, not required.
- **`codex.loop.serial-verdict`** additionally requires `recordExitCode === 0` (F6.8). It reads the last `STORY-001-01` line of `SPRINT-01/serial/wave1.verdicts.jsonl`; the extra `recorded_at` key is ignored by `validateVerdicts`.
- **Result.** `result` = PASS iff all of these hold:
  - `loop_transport` PASS and `enforcement_parity` PASS;
  - `verifyInstallExit === 0` and `doctorExit === 0`;
  - for `posture: 'human'`: `configWrites === 0`, and `configBefore`/`configAfter` deep-equal on `hooks.state` and `projects["<roomReal>"]`.

  `codex.loop.trust-posture` (INFO) names any violation.

**F6.7 `codex-loop.mjs` CLI deltas (extends M6:745-786).**
- **`run --container <C>`.**
  - `<C>/prepare.json` exists → run against that prepared room. `--codex-bin` comes from prepare.json, re-hashed; a mismatch exits 2.
  - `<C>` absent → scripted `prepare` into `<C>`, then run (needs `--codex-bin`, `--provider scripted`).
  - `<C>` exists without `prepare.json` → exit 2.
  - No `--container` → the default `path.resolve(CLI_ROOT, '..', '..', 'cg-cleanroom-<utc>-codexloop')`. From a nested worktree that is inside `OUTER/.worktrees` and refused, so worktree runs always pass `--container` (rule 20).
- **`--meta-repo <abs>`** (default `realpath(CLI_ROOT/..)`): the tree `codex.loop.meta-repo-untouched` watches. Worktree runs pass `--meta-repo /Users/ssuladze/Documents/Dev/ClearGate`.
- **Live mode reads under `CODEX_HOME` only** `config.toml` (the trust subtrees) and `sessions/**/rollout-*-<agent_id>.jsonl` (metering). `auth.json` and every other file are never read; the harness runs `login status` for the credential check.
- **Main guard**: realpath both sides (`fs.realpathSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) === fs.realpathSync(argv[1])`, the `dispatch_serial.mjs:516-523` pattern; flashcard 130). Import-safe.
- **`check --write-result`** writes -09's shape (`claude-baseline.mjs:766-776`): `{ story: 'STORY-058-13', command, ran_at, exit_code, status: 'green'|'red', reason }`. `close_sprint.mjs` Step 2.9 requires `status === 'green' && exit_code === 0`.
- `--help` prints usage and exits 0 (flashcard 9: codex-hooks' refusal is not copied).

**F6.8 -12's four plan deviations, as they reach -13.**
1. **storyId guard.** `dispatchSerial` throws on a verdict naming another story, and `record` exits 1 when `verdict.storyId ∉ wave.stories` (`dispatch_serial.mjs:486-491`). The record pipeline's JSON carries `"storyId":"STORY-001-01"`, and the fixture `waves.json` wave1 lists it. Test 20d checks both.
2. **Default seams.** -13 drives the CLI, never `dispatchSerial()`. The CLI's `next` uses the same `worktreeRegistered` and `pendingFlashcards`. Its repo root falls back to the git toplevel of the cwd: from inside `.worktrees/STORY-001-01` that is the worktree, which has no `plans/waves.json`, so `next`/`record` exit 2. Scripted execs therefore use workdir = room root. For the live run this is a recorded risk (Cross-story risks); the playbook gives no cwd.
3. **Exit codes.**
   - `next`: 0 dispatch/wave-complete, 3 halt, 4 process-flashcards, 2 usage.
   - `record`: 0 GREEN, 3 non-GREEN, 1 invalid (non-JSON, schema, or storyId not in the wave), 2 unreadable verdict file or corrupt log.

   `codex.loop.serial-verdict` requires exit 0 (F6.6). `next` prints a random `runId` (`mintRunId`, :100); the pipeline's literal `run-SPRINT-01-STORY-001-01-scripted` validates, because any non-empty string does.
4. **`planCodexPayload` JSDoc sentence.** No effect.

- -13 has **no twins**. At integration step 4 the INTEGRATE Developer does **not** `checkout --` either authored file.
- It runs `git -C <ow> merge --no-ff sprint/S-41` with them uncommitted, only if `git -C <ow> diff --name-only HEAD sprint/S-41 -- .cleargate/knowledge/clean-room-verification.md .cleargate/sprint-runs/SPRINT-41/codex-loop-evidence.md` is empty. Otherwise it stops, and the orchestrator commits the two files first in the merge lane.
- Doc text constraint: the `### Codex (STORY-058-13)` subsection contains no `npm publish` (the -08 doc test D2).
- **After integration**, run `node <cw>/scripts/clean-room/codex-loop.mjs check --evidence <ow>/.cleargate/sprint-runs/SPRINT-41/codex-loop-evidence.md --cli <cw> --ref story/STORY-058-13`.
  - Reason `stale` or `last scripted run FAIL` → re-run the scripted loop (F8 command) and append.
  - Reason `needs a live human-trust PASS run on the final artifact inputs` → the scripted run is current; no re-run.

**Scripted vs live.**
- **Provable with no owner login (scripted provider, pinned binary, scratch `CODEX_HOME`):**
  - every default and integration test above;
  - Run 1 and its integrated re-run, and QA-Verify's reproduction;
  - enforcement parity under `harness-exact-hash` trust: real hooks firing, real host denials and allows;
  - loop transport with a scripted model: real `spawn_agent`/`wait_agent` dispatches, real git steps in child shells, a DevOps-thread merge, the serial driver;
  - metering `unavailable` with an exact provider cross-check (`matchesProvider`).
- **Not provable without OD1 + STEP-L:**
  - a live model following the installed playbook (live loop transport);
